The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. It serves recruiters/hiring managers and the design-engineering community equally: personality on the surface, key information (experience, projects, contact) reachable in seconds.\n8\t\n9\t**Core Value:** The site must feel crafted and memorable (tactile, physics-based motion and strong visual identity) while key info stays instantly accessible, on mobile as much as desktop.\n10\t\n11\t### Constraints\n12\t\n13\t- **Tech stack**: Vite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind — required by motion-primitives and react-spring\n14\t- **Hosting**: GitHub Pages — static output only, needs Actions workflow or a built-output branch\n15\t- **Platform**: Mobile must be excellent — touch interactions, performance on phones\n16\t- **Accessibility/perf**: Assumed baseline: `prefers-reduced-motion` respected, good Lighthouse scores (to confirm with user)\n17\t- **Process**: Detailed documentation before coding; research and surface findings for tooling decisions\n18\t\n19\t<!-- GSD:project-end -->\n20\t\n21\t<!-- GSD:stack-start source:research/STACK.md -->\n22\t\n23\t## Technology Stack\n24\t\n25\t## Bottom Line\n26\t\n27\t## Recommended Stack\n28\t\n29\t### Core Framework\n30\t\n31\t| Technology | Version (verified 2026-10-08) | Purpose | Why | Confidence |\n32\t|------------|-------------------------------|---------|-----|------------|\n33\t| Vite | `^8.3.4` | Build/dev server | Current `latest`. `create-vite` 9.2.1 scaffolds it. Family name is `'Inter Variable'`. Realtime Colors takes any Google Font name, and Fontsource packages every Google font the same way, so whatever you pick there installs as `@fontsource-variable/<name>` (or `@fontsource/<name>` for non-variable). | HIGH |\n59\t| realtimecolors.com | web tool | Palette + type exploration | Design tool, not a dependency. See \"How to use the four tools\". | HIGH |\n60\t\n61\t### Supporting Libraries (recommended now)\n62\t\n63\t| Library | Version | Purpose | When to Use |\n64\t|---------|---------|---------|-------------|\n65\t| vite-imagetools | `^12.0.1` | Build-time resize + AVIF/WebP + srcset | Always, for the `images/` and `about-dj.jpg` assets. **Verified**: builds under Vite 8 and emits AVIF/WebP via `?w=400;800&format=avif;webp&as=picture`. Prefer 3 hand-inlined SVGs if only 3-4 are needed (zero deps). |\n68\t\n69\t### Development Tools\n70\t\n71\t| Tool | Purpose | Notes |\n72\t|------|---------|-------|\n73\t| oxlint (scaffold default) | Lint | `create-vite` 9.2.1 ships `oxlint` in `npm run lint`. Keep it. |\n74\t| @types/node | `vite.config.ts` path alias | Needed for `node:path` / `import.meta.dirname`. |\n75\t| Prettier + `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss` | Class sorting | Optional, recommended once Tailwind classes grow. |\n76\t\n77\t## Verified Bundle Costs (measured, gzipped JS, Vite 8 production build)\n78\t\n79\t| Build | JS gz | Delta vs. baseline |\n80\t|-------|-------|--------------------|\n81\t| Baseline: React 19.3 + Tailwind CSS (2.2 kB gz CSS) | 68.6 kB | n/a |\n82\t| + react-spring only (`useSpring`, `useTrail`, `useSprings`) | 85.8 kB | **+17.2 kB** |\n83\t| + motion with TextEffect, Tilt, Magnetic, `MotionConfig` | 121.5 kB | **+52.9 kB** |\n84\t| + both | 138.6 kB | **+70.0 kB** |\n85\t| Lenis (ReactLenis) alone | 74.2 kB | +5.6 kB |\n86\t| OGL (minimal Renderer/Program/Mesh) | 81.4 kB | +12.8 kB |\n87\t| GSAP core + ScrollTrigger | 112.7 kB | +44.1 kB |\n88\t| three + @react-three/fiber (one box) | 309.5 kB | **+240.9 kB** |\n89\t\n90\t- Coexistence costs about +17 kB beyond motion. That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. Do this only if Lighthouse demands it.\n92\t- Code-split below-the-fold sections with `React.lazy` so the hero ships alone. Any 3D must be lazy-loaded (see Gaps).\n93\t- Not measured: runtime frame rate on a real phone. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PNWHWG7Xmpsd3uGwHXi5DE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * True when the visitor chose a theme but storage refused it. Storage is the record of an\n12\t * explicit choice, so without this flag a blocked-storage visitor would look like one who\n13\t * never chose, and the next OS scheme change would silently undo their choice.\n14\t */\n15\tlet unsavedChoice = false\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n19\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only, and\n20\t * the OS scheme no longer overrides it.\n21\t */\n22\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n23\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n24\t  try {\n25\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n26\t    unsavedChoice = false\n27\t  } catch {\n28\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n29\t    unsavedChoice = true\n30\t  }\n31\t}\n32\t\n33\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n34\t\n35\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n36\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n40\t  try {\n41\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n42\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n43\t  } catch {\n44\t    return null\n45\t  }\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? 'dark' : 'light'\n51\t  } catch {\n52\t    return 'light'\n53\t  }\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\t/** Write the attribute without storing anything, and only when it would change. */\n57\tfunction syncAttribute(theme: Theme): void {\n58\t  if (readTheme() !== theme || !document.documentElement.hasAttribute('data-theme')) {\n59\t    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n60\t  }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t/**\n64\t * Subscribe to theme changes made outside the toggle: another tab changing or clearing the\n65\t * stored choice, and the OS scheme changing while nothing is stored. Both are folded into\n66\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n67\t * attribute changes for any reason. refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│        Static HTML shell  `index.html` / `404.html`          │\n│   inline theme script sets html[data-theme] before paint     │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               │ loads module\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Bootstrap  `src/main.tsx`  (createRoot, StrictMode, CSS)    │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Root  `src/app/App.tsx` → `src/app/Providers.tsx`           │\n│  MotionPreferenceProvider → MotionFeatures (LazyMotion)      │\n│  → ToastProvider                                             │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Page  `src/app/Page.tsx`  (single route, no router)         │\n│  reads `siteContent`, owns the scroll-spy (useActiveSection) │\n├──────────────────┬──────────────────┬───────────────────────┤\n│ Layout chrome    │ Sections          │ Overlays / nav        │\n│ `src/layout/`    │ `src/sections/`   │ `src/layout/PillNav`  │\n│ SiteHeader,      │ Hero, Experience, │ `src/sections/        │\n│ Footer, Section  │ Projects, About,  │  Projects/Project-    │\n│                  │ Skills, Education,│  Dialog`              │\n│                  │ Contact           │                       │\n└────────┬─────────┴────────┬──────────┴──────────┬────────────┘\n         │                  │                     │\n         ▼                  ▼                     ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Shared UI and motion  `src/components/ui/`, `src/anim/`,    │\n│  `src/components/motion-primitives/`                         │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Static content  `src/content/` (typed data, ImageAssets)    │\n│  + theme and clipboard helpers in `src/lib/`                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts React, loads the display font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Composes providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Motion preference, motion feature loader, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assembles sections in order and wires scroll-spy to nav | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content aggregate | Single export `siteContent` used by `Page` | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | Type contracts for all content entries | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Site header | Top nav, wordmark, theme toggle, resume link | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` |\n| Pill nav | Phone-size bottom navigation pill | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Section frame | Shared section wrapper with title and aside | `src/layout/Section.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | Picks the active section and locks it during programmatic scroll | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme logic | Reads, writes and syncs `data-theme` and `localStorage` | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Reads `prefers-reduced-motion` and exposes `reduced` | `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` |\n| Motion feature loader | Lazy-loads the `domMax` feature bundle after first paint | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time with a polite live region | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Projects crate | Horizontal scroll-snap sleeves; opens the project dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Project dialog | Native `<dialog>` with the project write-up | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, static, client-rendered React application. Content is typed data compiled into the bundle. There is no router, no store library and no server.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `src/app/Page.tsx` renders every section. The 404 page is a second static entry, `404.html`, built from the same app.\n- Content is data, not components. Section components take `entries`, `groups`, `projects`, `profile` or `links` props from `Page.tsx`.\n- Theme is a DOM attribute. `html[data-theme]` is the single source of truth; `src/lib/theme.ts` keeps it in sync with `localStorage` and `prefers-color-scheme`.\n- Motion is lazy. `motion` is loaded through `LazyMotion` with a dynamic `import('./features')`, so the feature bundle is a separate chunk. Reduced-motion visitors never download it.\n- Two animation libraries split by concern: `motion` for mount/exit, layout, scroll and gestures; `@react-spring/web` only for physics (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`).\n- Images are build-time transformed. `src/content/images.ts` imports images with `vite-imagetools` queries and exposes `ImageAsset` objects with AVIF/WebP `sources`.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / entry:**\n- Purpose: Start React and global CSS\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `index.html`, `404.html`\n- Contains: `createRoot` call, font and stylesheet imports, inline theme script in the HTML shells\n- Depends on: `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/styles/index.css`\n- Used by: Vite build (two rollup inputs in `vite.config.ts`)\n\n**App composition:**\n- Purpose: Providers and the single page\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim`, `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/content`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: Render each page section from content props\n- Location: `src/sections/` (one folder per section: `About`, `Contact`, `Education`, `Experience`, `Hero`, `Projects`, `Skills`)\n- Contains: section components; `Projects/` also holds `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx` and `project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section`, `src/components/ui`, `src/anim`, `src/content/schema` (types)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout and navigation:**\n- Purpose: Chrome around sections: header, footer, nav, skip link, container\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and sections\n\n**Shared UI and motion:**\n- Purpose: Reusable components and animation primitives\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink`, `ThemeToggle`, `Toast`, `useCopyEmail`, icons; the vendored `text-effect.tsx`; `anim/declarative/*` (`NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`); `anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/lib/utils`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The site's words, links and images as typed data\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `schema.ts` (types only), `src/assets/images/` (through `images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, and `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`\n\n**Lib and hooks:**\n- Purpose: Framework-free helpers and React hooks\n- Location: `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`), `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`)\n- Depends on: browser APIs only\n- Used by: components and layout\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`; the inline script reads `localStorage` key `theme` and sets `html[data-theme]` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. The module bundle loads and `src/main.tsx` calls `createRoot(...).render(<App />)`.\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which mount `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (starts `LazyMotion` with a loader), then `ToastProvider` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` (`src/content/index.ts`), derives the email from `contacts`, and renders sections in order: Hero, Experience, Projects, About, Skills, Education, Contact (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. After first paint, `MotionFeatures` schedules a `requestIdleCallback` (or a 1500 ms timer) to import `src/anim/features.ts`; `LazyMotion` then enables `m` components.\n\n### Scroll-spy and nav\n\n1. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. One `IntersectionObserver` watches each section element by id through a band under the sticky nav.\n3. `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` receive `activeId` and `onNavigate={lockTo}`; a click calls `lockTo`, which suppresses observer updates until `scrollend` or a timeout.\n\n### Project dialog\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` state. `Sleeve` calls `onOpen` (`src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx`).\n2. `ProjectDialog` (a sibling after the section, outside the inverted band) opens the native `<dialog>` for that index (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`).\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `applyTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`), which sets `html[data-theme]` first and then writes `localStorage`.\n2. `subscribeTheme` (used by `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`) listens for `MutationObserver` changes on `data-theme`, `storage` events from other tabs, and `prefers-color-scheme` changes while nothing is stored.\n\n### Copy email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` in `src/lib/copy.ts`, which uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText`.\n2. On success or failure it calls `show` from `useToast` (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`), which the `ToastProvider` renders.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state (`useState`, `useRef`) in `Page`, `Projects`, `ToastProvider` and `useActiveSection`\n- Theme state lives in the DOM attribute, not React state\n- No global store, no context beyond `ToastContext` and the motion preference context\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent` / content schema:**\n- Purpose: The one typed source of all page copy\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Pattern: Plain `as const satisfies` data objects per domain, with shared interfaces (`Profile`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`)\n\n**`ImageAsset`:**\n- Purpose: One responsive image with alt text and optional AVIF/WebP sources\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, `capitalOneLogo`, `spectrumLogo`, `symboticLogo`)\n- Pattern: Created by `vite-imagetools` imports; an empty `alt` marks decorative images (employer logos)\n\n**`ToastContext`:**\n- Purpose: Let any component show a toast without prop drilling\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`\n- Pattern: Context with a memoized `{ show }` API; one toast at a time, 4000 ms\n\n**Motion primitives:**\n- Purpose: Vendored and wrapped motion components\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`\n- Pattern: Thin wrappers over `motion/react`, using `m` components from `LazyMotion`; reduced-motion handled through `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n\n**Design tokens:**\n- Purpose: One token block per theme\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `scripts/lib/tokens.mjs`\n- Pattern: CSS custom properties on `html[data-theme]`; `band-invert` sections repeat the opposite theme's token block (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`)\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML entry (main site):**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: Browser request to `/` or the Pages base path\n- Responsibilities: Inline theme script, root `<div>`, module script to `src/main.tsx`\n\n**HTML entry (404):**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Same shell as `index.html` with the same inline theme script; styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html` and `404.html`\n- Responsibilities: Import the font and global CSS, mount `<App />` into `#root` under `StrictMode`\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build` and `npm run dev`\n- Responsibilities: Set `base` from `VITE_BASE`, register plugins, set the `@` alias, define the two rollup inputs\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers, no service worker.\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; `featuresStatus` module flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`; the DOM attribute `html[data-theme]`.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed in the entry chain. `src/anim/index.ts` re-exports most of `src/anim/`; keep feature code behind `src/anim/features.ts` so it stays out of the initial chunk.\n- **Static only:** No runtime fetch. Anything that needs a server (contact form, analytics, auth) would be a new architectural layer.\n- **Base path:** Asset URLs must use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (as in `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` for `resume.pdf`); hard-coded absolute paths break under the Pages base.\n- **Bundle budget:** `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` enforces a JS size budget (default 150000 bytes), so new imports in the initial graph count against it.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing `motion` feature code statically\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `domMax` or `features.ts` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Pulls the feature bundle into the initial chunk and defeats the lazy `LazyMotion` boundary in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`.\n**Do this instead:** Use `m` components under the existing `LazyMotion`, and import only from `@/anim` for the wrappers.\n\n### Hard-coded asset URLs\n\n**What happens:** `href=\"/resume.pdf\"` or similar absolute paths.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks when `VITE_BASE` changes (the Pages base is `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`, see `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`).\n**Do this instead:** `` href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}resume.pdf`} `` as in `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`.\n\n### Theme written to React state\n\n**What happens:** Storing the theme in component state and not in `html[data-theme]`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Splits the source of truth. The pre-paint inline scripts and the other tab sync in `src/lib/theme.ts` read the attribute only.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` from `src/lib/theme.ts`; read with `readTheme()` or subscribe with `subscribeTheme`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft in the browser. Blocked storage, missing `matchMedia`, failed motion chunk loads and clipboard failures all degrade without throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `try/catch` around `localStorage` in `src/lib/theme.ts`; in-memory choice fallback\n- `loadFeatures` in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` returns a never-settling promise on failure, so motion components stay static and no unhandled rejection occurs\n- `copyText` in `src/lib/copy.ts` guards `navigator.clipboard?.writeText`\n- No error boundary component was found under `src/`\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None. No console logging in runtime code.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. TypeScript types in `src/content/schema.ts`; content invariants are tested in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), polite live region in `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `prefers-reduced-motion` honored in `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, axe checks in the test suite.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":858,"cache_read_input_tokens":81026,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":858,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_014PvokPTtNBnnYQSb7db4Sd","agent_id":"a2158b048a4c3a442","session_id":"00166a7f-a983-41f1-b234-647719e9e318","uuid":"3b33a9f1-ba1c-4a8b-bef8-989fad7026a2","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:37:55.664Z","request_id":"req_011ChDZV4gsXXhmkWXJshSz3","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

Codebase Structure\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## Directory Layout\n\n```\n[repo root]/\n├── src/                      # All application code (React + TypeScript)\n│   ├── main.tsx              # Bootstrap: createRoot, fonts, global CSS\n│   ├── app/                  # App root, providers, single page\n│   ├── sections/             # One folder per page section\n│   ├── layout/ playwright.config.ts      # Playwright config\n├── tsconfig.json             # TS project references\n├── tsconfig.app.json         # App type check (src/)\n├── tsconfig.node.json        # Node-side    # Lint ignore list\n├── .prettierignore           # Prettier ignore list\n└── .gitignore\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Top-level composition\n- Contains: `App.tsx` (root), `Providers.tsx` (motion preference, motion features, toast), `Page.tsx` (section order and scroll-spy wiring)\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section, in page order\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `About/About.tsx`, `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Site chrome and the shared section frame\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Key files: `src/layout/Section.tsx` (wrapper every section uses), `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n\n**`src/components/ui/`:**\n- Purpose: Reusable UI pieces\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, and `icons/` (`ChevronLeftIcon.tsx`, `ChevronRightIcon.tsx`, `CloseIcon.tsx`, `DownloadIcon.tsx`, `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`)\n\n**`src/components/motion-primitives/`:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in motion source owned by the repo\n- Contains: `text-effect.tsx`\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: The whole motion layer\n- Contains: `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts` (lazy `domMax` chunk), `featuresStatus.ts`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `index.ts` (barrel), `declarative/` (`NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`, `ToastPresence.tsx`), `physics/` (`ThemeThumb.tsx`)\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts` (public barrel), `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All on-page words, links and images\n- Contains: `index.ts` (exports `siteContent`), `schema.ts` (types), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`\n- Key files: `src/content/schema.ts`, `src/content/images.ts`\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: React hooks\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy, click lock), `useTheme.ts` (`useSyncExternalStore` over `subscribeTheme`)\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Non-React helpers\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (theme attribute, storage, system sync), `copy.ts` (clipboard and mail), `utils.ts` (`cn`), `copy.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Global CSS\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind entry), `tokens.css` (theme token blocks), `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n\n**`src/types/`:**\n- Purpose: Ambient declarations\n- Contains: `imagetools.d.ts` (types for `?w=...&as=picture` imports)\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Source raster images (transformed at build time)\n- Contains: `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`\n\n**`tests/unit/`:**\n- Purpose: Unit and component tests (Vitest + jsdom)\n- Contains: `content.test.ts`, `content-inventory.test.ts`, `theme.test.ts`, `theme-script.test.ts`, `copy.test.ts`, `active-section.test.ts`, `layout.test.tsx`, `use-theme.test.tsx`, `motion-preference.test.tsx`, `tokens.test.ts`, `boundaries.test.ts`, `gates.test.ts`, `hero-parity.test.tsx`\n- Included by glob `tests/unit/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` in `vitest.config.ts`\n\n**`tests/e2e/`:**\n- Purpose: Browser tests against the built preview\n- Contains: `smoke.spec.ts`, `content.spec.ts`, `theme.spec.ts`, `nav.spec.ts`, `hero.spec.ts`, `copy-email.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`, `resume.spec.ts`, `not-found.spec.ts`, `wordmark.spec.ts`, `global-setup.ts`, `mobile/` (`pill.spec.ts`, `ten-second.spec.ts`)\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Gates run by `npm run verify`, and QA screenshot tooling\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Files served unchanged at the base path\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`\n- Referenced through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`, never hard-coded\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: Project planning: `PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `WINDOWS.md`, and per-phase folders under `phases/` (`01-decisions-and-documentation/`, `02-deployed-themed-skeleton/`, `03-content-complete-site/`, and later phases)\n- Generated: No (hand-written planning docs and generated summaries)\n- Committed: Yes (`.planning/milestone.lock` is git-ignored)\n\n**`.github/workflows/`:**\n- Purpose: CI (`ci.yml`) and Pages deploy (`deploy.yml`)\n- Generated: No\n- Committed: Yes\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `src/main.tsx`: JS bootstrap\n- `index.html`: main HTML shell\n- `404.html`: not-found HTML shell\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build, base path, aliases, rollup inputs\n- `vitest.config.ts`: unit test setup\n- `playwright.config.ts`: e2e setup\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: TS settings and the `@/*` path alias\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint rules\n- `package.json`: scripts (`verify` chain) and dependency pins\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section order and scroll-spy wiring\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy logic\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme sync logic\n- `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`: motion lazy-load boundary\n- `src/content/index.ts`: content aggregate\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: unit suites\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright suites\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup\n- `tests/fixtures/`: fixture components for gate tests\n\n**Build gates:**\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs`: run in the `verify` chain (see `package.json`)\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one component per file (`SiteHeader.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`)\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`); `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` is the exception that matches this rule\n- Non-component modules: kebab-case (`nav-items.ts`, `project-types.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts` is camelCase, `text-effect.tsx` is kebab-case under `motion-primitives`)\n- Content modules: lowercase noun (`profile.ts`, `projects.ts`, `contacts.ts`)\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`; Playwright `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Scripts: `check-*.mjs` for gates, `qa-*.mjs` for QA tools\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders: PascalCase matching the component (`src/sections/Projects/`)\n- Other source folders: lowercase (`src/anim/`, `src/lib/`, `src/layout/`)\n- Test folders: lowercase (`tests/unit/`, `tests/e2e/mobile/`)\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/` (for example `import { Section } from '@/layout/Section'`); `tsconfig.app.json` and `vite.config.ts` both define it\n- Relative imports (`./` and `../`) are used within a folder, for example `./ProjectDialog` in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New page section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (PascalCase folder and file)\n- Wire it into the section order in `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Add its id to `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` so the scroll-spy and nav include it\n- Content: a new domain file in `src/content/` and a new entry on `siteContent` in `src/content/index.ts`; add the type to `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for content and layout, `tests/e2e/` for behavior\n\n**New content entry (project, job, skill):**\n- Data: the matching file in `src/content/` (for example `src/content/projects.ts`)\n- Images: add to `src/content/images.ts` with a `vite-imagetools` import, and place the source in `src/assets/images/`\n- Validation: `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`\n\n**New reusable UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx`\n- Hook: `src/components/ui/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New motion wrapper:**\n- Implementation: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (uses `m` components)\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts`\n- Do not import `src/anim/features.ts` directly; it is the lazy chunk\n\n**New layout chrome:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`\n\n**New theme token or palette:**\n- Tokens: `src/styles/tokens.css` (one block per theme)\n- Motion tokens: `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Contrast check: add a pair to `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`\n\n**New hook:**\n- Implementation: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New helper    # Lint ignore list\n├── .prettierignore           # Prettier ignore list\n└── .gitignore\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Top-level composition\n- Contains: `App.tsx` `src/app/Providers.tsx`\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section, in page order\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `About/About.tsx`, `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Site chrome and the shared section frame\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`\n- Key files: `src/layout/Section.tsx` (wrapper every section uses), `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n\n**`src/components/ui/`:**\n- Purpose: Reusable UI pieces\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, and `icons/` (`ChevronLeftIcon.tsx`, `ChevronRightIcon.tsx`, `CloseIcon.tsx`, `DownloadIcon.tsx`, `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`)\n\n**`src/components/motion-primitives/`:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in motion source owned by the repo\n- Contains: `text-effect.tsx`\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: The whole motion layer\n- Contains: `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts` (lazy `domMax` chunk), `featuresStatus.ts`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `index.ts` (barrel), `declarative/` (`NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`, `ToastPresence.tsx`), `physics/` (`ThemeThumb.tsx`)\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts` (public barrel), `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All on-page words, links and images\n- Contains: `index.ts` (exports `siteContent`), `schema.ts` (types), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`\n- Key files: `src/content/schema.ts`, `src/content/images.ts`\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: React hooks\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy, click lock), `useTheme.ts` (`useSyncExternalStore` over `subscribeTheme`)\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Non-React helpers\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (theme attribute, storage, system sync), `copy.ts` (clipboard and mail), `utils.ts` (`cn`)\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Global CSS\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind entry), `tokens.css` (theme token blocks), `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n\n**`src/types/`:**\n- Purpose: Ambient declarations\n- Contains: `imagetools.d.ts` (types for `?w=...&as=picture` imports)\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Source raster images (transformed at build time)\n- Contains: `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`\n\n**`tests/unit/`:**\n- Purpose: Unit and component tests (Vitest + jsdom)\n- Contains: `content.test.ts`, `content-inventory.test.ts`, `theme.test.ts`, `theme-script.test.ts`, `copy.test.ts`, `active-section.test.ts`, `layout.test.tsx`, `use-theme.test.tsx`, `motion-preference.test.tsx`, `tokens.test.ts`, `boundaries.test.ts`, `gates.test.ts`, `hero-parity.test.tsx`\n- Included by the glob `tests/unit/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` in `vitest.config.ts`\n\n**`tests/e2e/`:**\n- Purpose: Browser tests against the built preview\n- Contains: `smoke.spec.ts`, `content.spec.ts`, `theme.spec.ts`, `nav.spec.ts`, `hero.spec.ts`, `copy-email.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`, `resume.spec.ts`, `not-found.spec.ts`, `wordmark.spec.ts`, `global-setup.ts`, `mobile/` (`pill.spec.ts`, `ten-second.spec.ts`)\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Gates run by `npm run verify`, and QA screenshot tooling\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-images.mjs` (all six run in `verify`), `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs` (QA screenshots), `contrast-pairs.json`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Files served unchanged at the base path\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`\n- Referenced through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`, never hard-coded\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: Project planning: `PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `WINDOWS.md`, and per-phase folders under `phases/` (`01-decisions-and-documentation/`, `02-deployed-themed-skeleton/`, `03-content-complete-site/`, and later phases)\n- Generated: No (hand-written planning docs and generated summaries)\n- Committed: Yes (`.planning/milestone.lock` is git-ignored)\n\n**`.github/workflows/`:**\n- Purpose: CI (`ci.yml`) and Pages deploy (`deploy.yml`)\n- Generated: No\n- Committed: Yes\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `src/main.tsx`: JS bootstrap\n- `index.html`: main HTML shell\n- `404.html`: not-found HTML shell\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build, base path, aliases, rollup inputs\n- `vitest.config.ts`: unit test setup\n- `playwright.config.ts`: e2e setup\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: TS settings and the `@/*` path alias\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint rules\n- `package.json`: scripts (`verify` chain) and dependency pins\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: section order and scroll-spy wiring\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy logic\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme sync logic\n- `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`: motion lazy-load boundary\n- `src/content/index.ts`: content aggregate\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: unit suites\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright suites\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup\n- `tests/fixtures/`: fixture components for gate tests\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one component per file (`SiteHeader.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`)\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useTheme.ts`, `useToast.ts`)\n- Non-component modules: kebab-case in most places (`nav-items.ts`, `project-types.ts`, `text-effect.tsx`); camelCase in `src/anim/` and `src/lib/` (`featuresStatus.ts`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `theme.ts`)\n- Content modules: lowercase noun (`profile.ts`, `projects.ts`, `contacts.ts`)\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`; Playwright `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Scripts: `check-*.mjs` for gates, `qa-*.mjs` for QA tools\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders: PascalCase matching the component (`src/sections/Projects/`)\n- Other source folders: lowercase (`src/anim/`, `src/lib/`, `src/layout/`)\n- Test folders: lowercase (`tests/unit/`, `tests/e2e/mobile/`)\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/` (for example `import { Section } from '@/layout/Section'`); `tsconfig.app.json` and `vite.config.ts` both define it\n- Relative imports (`./` and `../`) are used within a folder, for example `./ProjectDialog` in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New page section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (PascalCase folder and file)\n- Wire it into the section order in `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Add its id to `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` so the scroll-spy and nav include it\n- Content: a new domain file in `src/content/` and a new entry on `siteContent` in `src/content/index.ts`; add the type to `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for content and layout, `tests/e2e/` for behavior\n\n**New content entry (project, job, skill):**\n- Data: the matching file in `src/content/` (for example `src/content/projects.ts`)\n- Images: add to `src/content/images.ts` with a `vite-imagetools` import, and place the source in `src/assets/images/`\n- Validation: `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`\n\n**New reusable UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx`\n- Hook: `src/components/ui/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New motion wrapper:**\n- Implementation: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (uses `m` components)\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts`\n- Do not import `src/anim/features.ts` directly; it is the lazy chunk\n\n**New layout chrome:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`\n\n**New theme token or palette:**\n- Tokens: `src/styles/tokens.css` (one block per theme)\n- Motion tokens: `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Contrast check: add a pair to `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`\n\n**New hook:**\n- Implementation: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New helper (non-React):**\n- Implementation: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n- Shared class helper: `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn`)\n\n**New unit test:**\n- `tests/unit/<name>.test.ts` or `.test.tsx` (picked up by the Vitest glob automatically)\n\n**New e2e test:**\n- `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`; use `tests/e2e/mobile/` for mobile-only specs\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output, uploaded to GitHub Pages\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (git-ignored)\n\n**`node_modules/`:**\n- Purpose: Installed packages\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No\n\n**`test-results/`, `playwright-report/`, `playwright/.cache/`:**\n- Purpose: Playwright outputs\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (git-ignored)\n\n**`.shots/`:**\n- Purpose: Local QA screenshots and audit text\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (git-ignored)\n\n**`.planning/phases/`:**\n- Purpose: Phase plans, summaries, reviews and verification records\n- Generated: Mostly hand-written by GSD workflows\n- Committed: Yes\n\n---\n\n*Structure refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│        Static HTML shell  `index.html` / `404.html`          │\n│   inline theme script sets html[data-theme] before paint     │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Root  `src/app/App.tsx` → `src/app/Providers.tsx`           │\n│  MotionPreferenceProvider → MotionFeatures (LazyMotion)      │\n│  → ToastProvider (single route, no router)         │\n│  reads `siteContent`, owns the scroll-spy (useActiveSection) │\n├──────────────────┬──────────────────┬───────────────────────┤\n│ Layout chrome    │ Sections │\n└──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┘\n                               ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Static content  `src/content/` (typed data, ImageAssets)    │\n│  + theme and clipboard helpers in `src/lib/`                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts React, loads the display font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Composes providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Motion preference, motion feature loader, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assembles sections in order and wires scroll-spy to nav | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content aggregate | Single export `siteContent` used by `Page` | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | Type contracts for all content entries | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Site header | Top nav, wordmark, theme toggle, resume link | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` |\n| Pill nav | Phone-size bottom navigation pill | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Section frame | Shared section wrapper with title and aside | `src/layout/Section.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | Picks the active section and locks it during programmatic scroll | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme logic | Writes `data-theme` and `localStorage`, syncs with other tabs and the OS scheme | `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Theme hook | Exposes the current theme and a toggle to components | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Reads `prefers-reduced-motion` and exposes `reduced` | `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` |\n| Motion feature loader | Lazy-loads the `domMax` feature bundle after first paint | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time with a polite live region | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Projects crate | Horizontal scroll-snap sleeves; opens the project dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Project dialog | Native `<dialog>` with the project write-up | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, static, client-rendered React application. Content is typed data compiled into the bundle. There is no router, no store library and no server.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `src/app/Page.tsx` renders every section. The 404 page is a second static entry, `404.html`, built from the same app.\n- Content is data, not components. Section components take `entries`, `groups`, `projects`, `profile` or `links` props from `Page.tsx`.\n- Theme is a DOM attribute. `html[data-theme]` is the single source of truth; `src/lib/theme.ts` keeps it in sync with `localStorage` and `prefers-color-scheme`.\n- Motion is lazy. `motion` is loaded through `LazyMotion` with a dynamic `import('./features')`, so the feature bundle is a separate chunk. Reduced-motion visitors never download it.\n- Two animation libraries split by concern: `motion` for mount/exit, layout, scroll and gestures; `@react-spring/web` only for physics (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`).\n- Images are build-time transformed. `src/content/images.ts` imports images with `vite-imagetools` queries and exposes `ImageAsset` objects with AVIF/WebP `sources`.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / entry:**\n- Purpose: Start React and global CSS\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `index.html`, `404.html`\n- Contains: `createRoot` call, font and stylesheet imports, inline theme script in the HTML shells\n- Depends on: `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/styles/index.css`\n- Used by: Vite build (two rollup inputs in `vite.config.ts`)\n\n**App composition:**\n- Purpose: Providers and the single page\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim`, `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/content`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: Render each page section from content props\n- Location: `src/sections/` (one folder per section: `About`, `Contact`, `Education`, `Experience`, `Hero`, `Projects`, `Skills`)\n- Contains: section components; `Projects/` also holds `Sleeve.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx` and `project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section`, `src/components/ui`, `src/anim`, `src/content/schema` (types)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout and navigation:**\n- Purpose: Chrome around sections: header, footer, nav, skip link, container\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` and sections\n\n**Shared UI and motion:**\n- Purpose: Reusable components and animation primitives\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink`, `ThemeToggle`, `Toast`, `useCopyEmail`, icons; the vendored `text-effect.tsx`; `anim/declarative/*` (`NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`); `anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/lib/utils`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The site's words, links and images as typed data\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `schema.ts` (types only), `src/assets/images/` (through `images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, and `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`\n\n**Lib and hooks:**\n- Purpose: Framework-free helpers and React hooks\n- Location: `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`), `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`)\n- Depends on: browser APIs only (hooks also depend on React)\n- Used by: components and layout\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`; the inline script reads `localStorage` key `theme` and sets `html[data-theme]` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. The module bundle loads and `src/main.tsx` calls `createRoot(...).render(<App />)`.\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which mount `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (starts `LazyMotion` with a loader), then `ToastProvider` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` (`src/content/index.ts`), derives the email from `contacts`, and renders sections in order: Hero, Experience, Projects, About, Skills, Education, Contact (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. After first paint, `MotionFeatures` schedules a `requestIdleCallback` (or a 1500 ms timer) to import `src/anim/features.ts`; `LazyMotion` then enables `m` components.\n\n### Scroll-spy and nav\n\n1. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. One `IntersectionObserver` watches each section element by id through a band under the sticky nav.\n3. `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` receive `activeId` and `onNavigate={lockTo}`; a click calls `lockTo`, which suppresses observer updates until `scrollend` or a timeout.\n\n### Project dialog\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` state. `Sleeve` calls `onOpen` (`src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx`).\n2. `ProjectDialog` (a sibling after the section, outside the inverted band) opens the native `<dialog>` for that index (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`).\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. The toggle reads and flips the theme through `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`), which calls `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`. `applyTheme` sets `html[data-theme]` first and then writes `localStorage`.\n2. `useTheme` uses `useSyncExternalStore` over `subscribeTheme`, which listens for `MutationObserver` changes on `data-theme`, `storage` events from other tabs, and `prefers-color-scheme` changes while nothing is stored.\n\n### Copy email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` in `src/lib/copy.ts`, which uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText`.\n2. On success or failure it calls `show` from `useToast` (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`), which the `ToastProvider` renders.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state (`useState`, `useRef`) in `Page`, `Projects`, `ToastProvider` and `useActiveSection`\n- Theme state lives in the DOM attribute, read through `useSyncExternalStore`, not in React state\n- No global store, no context beyond `ToastContext` and the motion preference context\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent` / content schema:**\n- Purpose: The one typed source of all page copy\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Pattern: Plain `as const satisfies` data objects per domain, with shared interfaces (`Profile`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `ImageAsset`, `DateRange`)\n\n**`ImageAsset`:**\n- Purpose: One responsive image with alt text and optional AVIF/WebP sources\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`aboutPhoto`, `capitalOneLogo`, `spectrumLogo`, `symboticLogo`)\n- Pattern: Created by `vite-imagetools` imports; an empty `alt` marks decorative images (employer logos)\n\n**`ToastContext`:**\n- Purpose: Let any component show a toast without prop drilling\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`\n- Pattern: Context with a memoized `{ show }` API; one toast at a time, 4000 ms\n\n**Motion primitives:**\n- Purpose: Vendored and wrapped motion components\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`\n- Pattern: Thin wrappers over `motion/react`, using `m` components from `LazyMotion`; reduced-motion handled through `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n\n**Design tokens:**\n- Purpose: One token block per theme\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `scripts/lib/tokens.mjs`\n- Pattern: CSS custom properties on `html[data-theme]`; `band-invert` sections repeat the opposite theme's token block (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`)\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML entry (main site):**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: Browser request to `/` or the Pages base path\n- Responsibilities: Inline theme script, root `<div>`, module script to `src/main.tsx`\n\n**HTML entry (404):**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Same shell as `index.html` with the same inline theme script; styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html` and `404.html`\n- Responsibilities: Import the font and global CSS, mount `<App />` into `#root` under `StrictMode`\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build` and `npm run dev`\n- Responsibilities: Set `base` from `VITE_BASE`, register plugins, set the `@` alias, define the two rollup inputs\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers, no service worker.\n- **Global state:** Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; `featuresStatus` module flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`; the DOM attribute `html[data-theme]`.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed in the entry chain. `src/anim/index.ts` is a barrel over `src/anim/`; keep feature code behind `src/anim/features.ts` so it stays out of the initial chunk.\n- **Static only:** No runtime fetch. Anything that needs a server (contact form, analytics, auth) would be a new architectural layer.\n- **Base path:** Asset URLs must use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (as in `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` for `resume.pdf`); hard-coded absolute paths break under the Pages base.\n- **Bundle budget:** `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` enforces a JS size budget (default 150000 bytes), so new imports in the initial graph count against it.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing `motion` feature code statically\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `domMax` or `features.ts` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Pulls the feature bundle into the initial chunk and defeats the lazy `LazyMotion` boundary in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`.\n**Do this instead:** Use `m` components under the existing `LazyMotion`, and import only from `@/anim` for the wrappers.\n\n### Hard-coded asset URLs\n\n**What happens:** `href=\"/resume.pdf\"` or similar absolute paths.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks when `VITE_BASE` changes (the Pages base is `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`, see `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`).\n**Do this instead:** `` href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}resume.pdf`} `` as in `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`.\n\n### Theme written to React state\n\n**What happens:** Storing the theme in component state and not in `html[data-theme]`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Splits the source of truth. The pre-paint inline scripts and the other-tab sync in `src/lib/theme.ts` read the attribute only.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` through `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`); read with `readTheme()` or subscribe with `subscribeTheme` from `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft in the browser. Blocked storage, missing `matchMedia`, failed motion chunk loads and clipboard failures all degrade without throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `try/catch` around `localStorage` in `src/lib/theme.ts`; in-memory choice fallback\n- `loadFeatures` in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` returns a never-settling promise on failure, so motion components stay static and no unhandled rejection occurs\n- `copyText` in `src/lib/copy.ts` guards `navigator.clipboard?.writeText`\n- No error boundary component was found under `src/`\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None. No console logging in runtime code.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. TypeScript types in `src/content/schema.ts`; content invariants are tested in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), polite live region in `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `prefers-reduced-motion` honored in `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, and a QA gate that counts axe serious violations (`tests/unit/gates.test.ts`).\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6725,"cache_read_input_tokens":98418,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6725,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_014PvokPTtNBnnYQSb7db4Sd","agent_id":"a2158b048a4c3a442","session_id":"00166a7f-a983-41f1-b234-647719e9e318","uuid":"a95924c5-748c-4ef5-9d9d-419710f8ecf8","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:39:07.307Z","request_id":"req_011ChDZaotYgXCaQsrpjVTob","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}
